# Russia's War on Ukraine: Material Quotes, 12 November 2026 to 25 November 2026

Digest for 25 November 2026. Period: 2026-11-12 to 2026-11-25 (14 days, by publication date, UTC). 787 current quotes from BBC News, The Kyiv Independent, Reuters; 20 older quotes hidden as superseded.

Quotes are copied verbatim from the source articles. Section summaries are generated by an LLM.
